  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Washington, leader of America (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: America
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Minuteman, Replacing Musketman (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Monitor, Replacing Ironclad (Requires technology: Industrialization)
        - Unique Building: Independence Hall, Replacing Royal Collection (Requires technology: Architecture)
        - Unique Building: Homestead, Replacing Stable (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Civilization Ability: Military Land Units start with the Sentry Promotion. Tile purchase cost is reduced by 25% and is reset upon founding a City. +20 Production when purchasing tiles, scaling with Era. Can purchase tiles owned by others.
      - Leader: Washington
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Robotics: An important technology for the Space Race, Robotics allows you to build the Spaceship Factory, a building which speeds the construction of Spaceship parts. It also allows you to build the Missile Cruiser, a Naval Unit capable of carrying 3 Missiles and spotting enemy Submarines.
        Completing it would unlock: Nanotechnology, Particle Physics
      - The Internet: Increases Tourism output of all cities by 50%, helping you advance toward a Cultural Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Nanotechnology, Particle Physics, Globalization
      - Nuclear Fusion: Allows you to build the SS Booster, a component necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Future Tech
      
      ## Policies
      - Co-Prosperity Sphere (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Co-Prosperity Sphere: Bullying City-States no longer reduces Influence, revokes Quests, or cancels Pledges of Protection. Doing so decreases the Influence of all other Civs with the bullied City-State by 10%, and increases your Influence by 10%.
      - Cult of Personality (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Cult of Personality: +50% Tourism to civilizations fighting a common enemy. 50% of your highest Warscore counts as a Tourism Modifier with all Civilizations. Free Great Person of your choice.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Mercantilism (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Science and Culture from Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, and Stock Exchanges.
        - * Cities earn +10 Science when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Majesty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Specialists in Capital consume half the normal amount of Food.
        - * Palace Gardens built in Capital (+5 Food, +25% Great Person Rate, -2 Unhappiness from Urbanization, 1 Great Work of Writing slot, 1 Writer Slot).
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Honor (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Military Units gain the Conscription Promotion (+10% Combat Strength).
        - * Supply-free military Units spawn near Cities that reach (or have already reached) a multiple of 10 Citizens for the first time.
        - * -25% War Weariness.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Tribute (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * A Settler appears near the Capital.
        - * Gain Culture in Capital equal to 25% of the Gold Tribute demanded from City-States (50% if all Authority policies are adopted).
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 8
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 10
        - WarBias: 10
        - HostileBias: 10
        - WarmongerHate: 1
        - NeutralBias: 5
        - FriendlyBias: 8
        - GuardedBias: 10
        - AfraidBias: 1
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Friendliness: 8
        - WorkWithWillingness: 9
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - Loyalty: 9
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 1
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 10
        - MinorCivWarBias: 10
        - DenounceWillingness: 10
        - Forgiveness: 1
        - Meanness: 10
        - Neediness: 8
        - Chattiness: 5
        - DeceptiveBias: 1
        - Rationale: Maintaining our hyper-aggressive persona is critical. We are at war with Persia (again after peace expired) and Russia, and we must ensure the AI rejects any peace offers, continues sieging Sukhothai (Siam's capital at 48% health), and prioritizes offensive operations against Persian cities and Russian naval/air assets. High WarBias, HostileBias, Meanness, and low Forgiveness keep the AI uncompromising. High MinorCivHostileBias/WarBias ensures we continue attacking hostile city-states (Shanghai, Palatium) allied to Russia. Our vassals (Arabia, Zulus, Byzantium) are treated positively via existing relationship modifiers. No changes needed—this persona is optimized for total war and domination victory pursuit while countering Russia's science lead.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Robotics
        - Rationale: We are currently researching Alternative Energy (5 turns left). Robotics is a critical technology for both military and science victory: it unlocks the Spaceship Factory (speeds spaceship part production) and the powerful Missile Cruiser naval unit. While The Internet provides tourism, our cultural influence is not our primary path—we need to keep pace with Russia's science progress (they have a 16% lead and have completed two spaceship parts). Robotics is a prerequisite for further space techs (Nanotechnology, Particle Physics) and will bolster our naval dominance against Russia and Persia. It aligns with our dual focus on military supremacy and keeping the science victory option open as a backup.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Mercantilism (Policy)
        - Rationale: Our next policy is in 5 turns. Continuing the Industry branch with Mercantilism provides +3% Science and Culture from economic buildings (Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, Stock Exchanges) and +10 Science when constructing buildings (scaling with Era). This synergizes with our vast empire's high production and building construction, boosting science output to close the gap with Russia and funding our military through enhanced gold and science yields. It's a direct upgrade to our economic engine without diverting from our military focus. After Mercantilism, we should consider Entrepreneurship (next in Industry) for additional production and gold, or Colonialism in Imperialism for science/culture from military buildings.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Siam
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Siam is Persia's vassal and their capital Sukhothai is at 48% health under our siege. As an extension of Persia, they must be destroyed. Maximum hostility ensures our AI continues the assault on Sukhothai.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Shanghai
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: City-State Shanghai is a permanent ally of Russia and a friend of Persia. It is at war with us and our vassals. Capturing or neutralizing Shanghai would significantly weaken Russia's city-state alliance network and deny them valuable votes and resources. Maximum hostility ensures our AI targets it for military action.
        - UpdatedTurn: 416
        
        ### Tyre
        - Public: -10
        - Private: -10
        - Rationale: City-State Tyre switched its permanent alliance from us to our vassal (Zulus). This represents a loss of direct control. Maintaining a minor negative modifier (-10) signals to our AI a guarded stance, encouraging pressure tactics (bullying) rather than treating it as a fully friendly ally. This adjustment aligns with our high MinorCivHostileBias and Meanness persona.
        - UpdatedTurn: 420
        
        ### Palatium
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: City-State Palatium (Player 34) declared war on us, breaking our pledge, and is a permanent ally of Russia. As an active aggressor and key node in our enemies' diplomatic network, maximum hostility ensures our AI prioritizes military action against it, reducing Russia's diplomatic victory prospects and securing our flanks. This aligns with our high MinorCivHostileBias/WarBias persona and total war strategy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 427
        
        ### Arabia
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: Arabia is our loyal, capitulated vassal. They are fighting Persia and Russia alongside us, and our trade routes with them are vital for gold income. Positive modifiers ensure continued military coordination and prevent conflict. No change.
        - UpdatedTurn: 428
        
        ### The Zulus
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: The Zulus are our capitulated vassal. They are producing units and at war with our enemies. Positive modifiers ensure friendly treatment and military integration. No change.
        - UpdatedTurn: 428
        
        ### Byzantium
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: Byzantium is our voluntary vassal and has a peace treaty with Persia (temporary). They are a valuable ally against Russia. Positive values maintain alliance and ensure our AI coordinates militarily. No change.
        - UpdatedTurn: 428
        
        ### Persia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Persia remains our primary war target. The peace treaty expired, and we are back at war (Turn 444). We must ensure our AI maintains maximum hostility to continue military operations against Persian cities (Jinjan, Borazjan, Trebizond, Dakyanus) and reject any peace offers. No change needed—our current modifiers are already at -100, which is correct.
        - UpdatedTurn: 446
        
        ### Russia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Russia is our main competitor—they hold two capitals and have completed a second spaceship part. Their military strength is overwhelming (95k vs our 44k), but we must maintain maximum hostility to continue naval pressure, upgrade units, and reject peace. We are at war with them, and they are advancing on science victory. Our AI must prioritize espionage against them and prepare for eventual assault on Moscow and Attila's Court. No change needed—modifiers already at -100.
        - UpdatedTurn: 446
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Our core strategy remains Conquest, but we must intensify pressure on Russia's science victory path. Russia has completed a second spaceship part and has a massive military (95k). We will increase UseNuke to 85 (higher per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes when conditions are met) and maintain high Air, Naval, and Science flavors to close the tech gap and maintain dominance. Offense at 100 ensures relentless assault on Persia's remaining cities (Jinjan, Borazjan, Trebizond, Dakyanus) and keeps the siege on Siam's capital Sukhothai. High Espionage (100) is critical to counter Russian tech stealing and protect our own cities. Production, Gold, and Science at 100 sustain our massive military, deficit spending, and research toward Robotics (next tech). We lower CityDefense slightly to 80 and Defense to 70 to shift more focus to offensive operations, but maintain high Antiair (90) and MilitaryTraining (90) for quality. Our vassals (Arabia, Zulus, Byzantium) are holding fronts; we will continue to leverage their units. No change to Recon, Airlift, or Expansion as we have enough cities and focus on conquest.
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest
        
        ### Flavors
        - Offense: 100
        - Defense: 70
        - Mobilization: 90
        - CityDefense: 80
        - MilitaryTraining: 90
        - Recon: 0
        - Ranged: 80
        - Mobile: 80

        - UseNuke: 85
        - Naval: 100
        - NavalRecon: 100
        - NavalGrowth: 50
        - NavalTileImprovement: 50
        - Air: 100

        - Airlift: 0
        - Expansion: 0
        - Growth: 80
        - TileImprovement: 80
        - Infrastructure: 100
        - Production: 100
        - WaterConnection: 50
        - Gold: 100

        - Culture: 0
        - Happiness: 100
        - GreatPeople: 30
        - Wonder: 0

        - VotesNeeded: 42

        - ActiveResolutions:
          - American Sphere of Influence: Mogadishu:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Global Peace Accords:
            - Description: Unit maintenance costs are raised by 25% Gold. 2.0x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 0.5x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: 431
          - Ban Luxury: Amber:
            - Description: A Luxury resource is designated as immoral, endangered, or otherwise inappropriate for use by the general population. As long as the ban is in place, Civilizations receive no Happiness or Monopoly benefits from this Luxury resource.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Hanoi:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Russian Sphere of Influence: Antananarivo:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Russian Sphere of Influence: Shanghai:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Holmul:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Travel Ban:
            - Description: Restricts international travel between Civilizations. Reduces the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 35%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Liberation:
            - Description: All Master-Vassal relationships are immediately and peacefully annulled.
            - Proposer: Russia
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.

        You, Washington (leader of America, Player 6), are making strategic decisions after turn 448.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Washington, leader of America (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: America
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Minuteman, Replacing Musketman (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Monitor, Replacing Ironclad (Requires technology: Industrialization)
        - Unique Building: Independence Hall, Replacing Royal Collection (Requires technology: Architecture)
        - Unique Building: Homestead, Replacing Stable (Requires technology: Chivalry)
        - Civilization Ability: Military Land Units start with the Sentry Promotion. Tile purchase cost is reduced by 25% and is reset upon founding a City. +20 Production when purchasing tiles, scaling with Era. Can purchase tiles owned by others.
      - Leader: Washington
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Robotics: An important technology for the Space Race, Robotics allows you to build the Spaceship Factory, a building which speeds the construction of Spaceship parts. It also allows you to build the Missile Cruiser, a Naval Unit capable of carrying 3 Missiles and spotting enemy Submarines.
        Completing it would unlock: Nanotechnology, Particle Physics
      - The Internet: Increases Tourism output of all cities by 50%, helping you advance toward a Cultural Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Nanotechnology, Particle Physics, Globalization
      - Nuclear Fusion: Allows you to build the SS Booster, a component necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Future Tech
      
      ## Policies
      - Co-Prosperity Sphere (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Co-Prosperity Sphere: Bullying City-States no longer reduces Influence, revokes Quests, or cancels Pledges of Protection. Doing so decreases the Influence of all other Civs with the bullied City-State by 10%, and increases your Influence by 10%.
      - Cult of Personality (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Cult of Personality: +50% Tourism to civilizations fighting a common enemy. 50% of your highest Warscore counts as a Tourism Modifier with all Civilizations. Free Great Person of your choice.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Mercantilism (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Science and Culture from Markets, Caravansaries, Customs Houses, Banks, and Stock Exchanges.
        - * Cities earn +10 Science when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Majesty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Specialists in Capital consume half the normal amount of Food.
        - * Palace Gardens built in Capital (+5 Food, +25% Great Person Rate, -2 Unhappiness from Urbanization, 1 Great Work of Writing slot, 1 Writer Slot).
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Honor (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * All Military Units gain the Conscription Promotion (+10% Combat Strength).
        - * Supply-free military Units spawn near Cities that reach (or have already reached) a multiple of 10 Citizens for the first time.
        - * -25% War Weariness.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Tribute (Continuing Authority Branch):
        - * A Settler appears near the Capital.
        - * Gain Culture in Capital equal to 25% of the Gold Tribute demanded from City-States (50% if all Authority policies are adopted).
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Enlightenment
        - Rationale: Russia is leading in technology and actively building spaceship parts. Enlightenment provides a free technology immediately, which we can use to acquire Robotics or another high-tier tech to close the science gap. It also grants +1 Happiness from Universities, bolstering our empire's stability during this total war.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Robotics
        - Rationale: Robotics is a critical technology that unlocks the Spaceship Factory to boost our production of spaceship parts (should we need a backup science victory) and the Missile Cruiser naval unit to maintain naval dominance against Russia. It is also a prerequisite for advanced space-race technologies.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: We are facing a severe gold deficit of -1378 GPT, which threatens to bankrupt our massive military and penalize our science. While Persia is a target, they are currently militarily weak (17k strength) and not a threat to win. Russia is our main threat, nearing a Science Victory. Adjusting our relationship with Persia to a pragmatic -30/-30 will encourage our AI to accept a highly lucrative peace treaty with them, extracting massive gold and GPT tribute to fund our total war against Russia.
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -30
        - TargetID: 0
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - Defense: 70
          - Naval: 100
          - Nuke: 95
          - GreatPeople: 30
          - NavalRecon: 100
          - Mobilization: 90
          - Air: 100
          - Offense: 100
          - Spaceship: 0
          - Happiness: 100
          - WaterConnection: 50
          - Antiair: 90
          - NavalGrowth: 50
          - Infrastructure: 100
          - TileImprovement: 80
          - Ranged: 80
          - Culture: 0
          - Diplomacy: 95
          - Religion: 5
          - Mobile: 80
          - UseNuke: 85
          - Expansion: 0
          - Gold: 100
          - Recon: 0
          - NavalTileImprovement: 50
          - AirCarrier: 100
          - Airlift: 0
          - Science: 60
          - CityDefense: 80
          - Growth: 80
          - MilitaryTraining: 90
          - Espionage: 100
          - Production: 100
      - rationale: We must urgently address our -1378 GPT deficit to prevent science penalties and unit disbandment. By maintaining Gold and Production at 100 but lowering Science to 60, we encourage our AI to run 'Infinite Project: Wealth' instead of 'Infinite Project: Research' in our highly productive cities, balancing the budget. We keep Offense at 100, Espionage at 100, and Nuke/UseNuke high to continue our aggressive stance against Russia and defend against their science lead, while using our massive air force (Air at 100) to dominate the skies.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 70803
    - reasoningTokens: 8462
    - outputTokens: 563
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Current Geopolitics**
            
            My focus is on our current standing, particularly our close proximity to Russia in score. I'm examining the broader geopolitical landscape, noting Persia's position as well.
            
            **Assessing Domination Strategy**
            
            I'm verifying our current capital control for a Domination Victory. It appears we hold five original capitals, well past the 62% threshold needed, with Russia controlling only two. Our analysis confirms we've secured enough original capitals to achieve this win condition.
            
            **Capturing Remaining Capitals**
            
            I've identified the original capitals controlled by Russia and Persia. To secure a Domination Victory, I'll need to capture or vassalize both of their capitals.
            
            **Defending Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm urgently analyzing Russia's "Global Liberation" resolution; its passage would cripple our vassal network. I'm also assessing their rapid scientific advancement and our own tourism deficit against them.
            
            **Fixing Financial Woes**
            
            I'm addressing our severe gold deficit, likely due to extensive military upkeep and ongoing wars. Establishing the remaining four trade routes is my immediate priority to stabilize our economy.
            
            **Developing New Technologies**
            
            My current focus is on our technological advancement. We are researching Alternative Energy, with Robotics next on the agenda, aiming to boost our capabilities quickly.
            
            **Revising Economic Strategy**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our economic priorities, specifically the stark gold deficit. The current policy leaning towards Mercantilism needs reassessment given the significant unit maintenance costs exacerbated by the "Global Peace Accords" resolution and our ongoing total war. I need to explore avenues for immediate gold generation, potentially through increased trade with vassals and adjusting city flavors.
            
            **Securing Economic Stability**
            
            My immediate concern is our dire gold situation and negative GPT. I'm investigating policy options for immediate gold generation and significant GPT increases. Mercantilism's potential is being re-examined alongside trade route and city flavor adjustments.
            
            **Researching Future Policies**
            
            I'm prioritizing future policy research, evaluating Entrepreneurship's massive GPT and production boost potential. Enlightenment's free tech is also incredibly appealing given Russia's scientific lead, offering a significant speed advantage.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Options**
            
            I'm reviewing the policy trees and considering Enlightenment for its free tech, especially since Russia is ahead scientifically. I need to confirm its prerequisites, specifically regarding Rationalism policies, before making a decision to help close that tech gap.
            
            **Addressing Economic Crisis**
            
            I'm scrutinizing our severe gold deficit of -1378 GPT, focusing on military upkeep as a primary cause due to our extensive unit count. I need to find immediate solutions to generate gold and boost our GPT, possibly through trade adjustments.
            
            **Choosing New Policies**
            
            I am comparing Enlightenment, Entrepreneurship, and Mercantilism to address our economic and tech deficits. Enlightenment offers a free tech to catch up scientifically, while Entrepreneurship promises significant GPT and production boosts from tiles. Mercantilism's bonuses are being re-evaluated for their current utility.
            
            **Choosing New Policy**
            
            I am now prioritizing Enlightenment for its free technology, seeing it as the best way to counter Russia's scientific lead. The happiness boost is a welcome secondary benefit.
            
            **Researching Critical Technologies**
            
            I'm prioritizing Robotics next. It grants access to powerful military units, essential for both offensive and defensive maneuvers, and the Spaceship Factory, which could be critical if we need to pivot to a science victory or block Russia.
            
            **Defending Vassal Network**
            
            I'm focusing on the immediate threat of Russia's "Global Liberation" resolution which would annul our vassals. I'm strategizing delegate votes, aiming to secure a 'No' vote on that and a 'Yes' on "Decolonization" to counter Russia's city-state alliances and protect our diplomatic standings.
            
            **Influencing World Congress**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on influencing the upcoming World Congress vote. I'm strategizing how to best leverage our existing diplomatic stances and relationships to ensure Russia's resolutions are opposed and ours, like "Decolonization," are passed. This involves scrutinizing city-state alliances, particularly those with Russia, to understand delegate distribution and identify potential avenues for manipulation.
            
            **Prioritizing Gold Generation**
            
            I'm intensely focused on our dire GPT deficit, exacerbated by high unit maintenance and unresolved trade routes. My priority is securing Gold and Production in city management to stabilize our economy and address the zero gold reserves.
            
            **Adjusting City Priorities**
            
            I'm shifting focus to city project priorities. Given the critical gold deficit, I will slightly lower the Science flavor and maintain the Gold flavor at 100 to encourage cities to prioritize Wealth projects over Research, directly addressing our zero gold reserves and severe GPT.
            
            **Prioritizing Economic Recovery**
            
            I'm recognizing that resolving our massive gold deficit is the absolute fastest way to restore our science output, which is currently suffering a significant penalty. Therefore, prioritizing gold generation through city flavors, even at the cost of some immediate science, is the most critical path forward.
            
            **Adjusting City Priorities**
            
            I'm re-calibrating city project priorities to aggressively tackle our gold deficit. By shifting focus towards wealth generation, even temporarily sacrificing some science, I aim to stabilize our economy and subsequently restore our scientific output.
            
            **Assessing Bomber Readiness**
            
            I am evaluating our bomber deployment across tactical zones, noting the presence of Stealth and Heavy Bombers in multiple strategic locations, indicating readiness for offensive operations.
            
            **Deploying Air Superiority**
            
            My focus is on our overwhelming air power. We possess a formidable fleet of 16 Stealth Bombers, 6 Jet Fighters, 40 Heavy Bombers, and 77 Zeros, ready for deployment across various strategic locations, ensuring air dominance.
            
            **Analyzing Air Dominance**
            
            I'm realizing our massive air force, with 139 aircraft, is the primary driver of our exorbitant gold maintenance costs, especially with the Global Peace Accords increasing unit upkeep. Russia's air force is negligible in comparison, giving us absolute air superiority. However, this advantage is threatened by our dire gold deficit.
            
            **Securing Gold Through Peace**
            
            I'm reassessing our war with Persia. Given our severe gold deficit, I realize a peace treaty could provide immediate and substantial financial relief through tribute and ongoing payments, instantly solving our GPT crisis.
            
            **Securing Peace and Funds**
            
            I'm now prioritizing peace with Persia. Strategically, it's a brilliant move that instantly resolves our severe GPT deficit through tribute. This allows me to concentrate our overwhelming military, especially our air force, solely on Russia and their imminent science victory.
            
            **Evaluating Peace with Persia**
            
            My current assessment leans towards a peace treaty with Persia as the most advantageous strategic maneuver. This action will instantly alleviate our critical GPT deficit through tribute, freeing our substantial military might to confront Russia's scientific victory threat.
            
            **Prioritizing Peace Strategy**
            
            I'm focusing on the strategic imperative of peace with Persia. This immediate action promises to inject vital gold into our economy, allowing my full attention to be redirected towards neutralizing Russia's science victory push.
            
            **Implementing Peace Accord**
            
            I'm now actively working to establish peace with Persia. This vital diplomatic step is intended to immediately address our dire GPT situation, enabling a consolidated focus on countering Russia's imminent science victory.
            
            **Adjusting Persia's Diplomacy**
            
            I'm reassessing our diplomatic stance with Persia. Given our prior inflexibility, I'm adjusting our relationship parameters to enable a more pragmatic approach to peace, specifically to secure tribute and address our GPT crisis before confronting Russia.
            
            **Negotiating Peace Terms**
            
            I am adjusting my diplomatic approach with Persia, moving from inflexibility towards pragmatism. My aim is to secure a lucrative peace, drawing tribute to resolve our GPT crisis before focusing our full attention on Russia's impending science victory.
            
            **Implementing Policy and Tech**
            
            I am implementing Enlightenment as our next policy to counter Russia's tech lead and boost happiness. Robotics is our chosen technology to unlock crucial military and science victory components, securing our future options.
            
            **Securing Immediate Gains**
            
            I am now prioritizing pragmatic diplomacy with Persia to secure substantial tribute, directly addressing our GPT deficit. This will enable us to fully commit our air superiority and military might towards countering Russia's imminent science victory. Simultaneously, I've adjusted city flavors to prioritize wealth generation over science, reinforcing economic stability.
            
            **Adjusting AI Priorities**
            
            I am recalibrating AI priorities to reflect our current strategic needs, heavily weighting offense, defense, and espionage. My focus is on ensuring our nuclear capabilities remain deterrent, while maximizing air and naval dominance. I'm also prioritizing diplomatic efforts to counter Russia's global influence and secure our happiness.

          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: HvTS1wUnNIZh3V51
          - toolName: set-research
          - input:
            - Technology: Robotics
            - Rationale: Robotics is a critical technology that unlocks the Spaceship Factory to boost our production of spaceship parts (should we need a backup science victory) and the Missile Cruiser naval unit to maintain naval dominance against Russia. It is also a prerequisite for advanced space-race technologies.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: 0FlA3OmUIygr66t2
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Rationale: We are facing a severe gold deficit of -1378 GPT, which threatens to bankrupt our massive military and penalize our science. While Persia is a target, they are currently militarily weak (17k strength) and not a threat to win. Russia is our main threat, nearing a Science Victory. Adjusting our relationship with Persia to a pragmatic -30/-30 will encourage our AI to accept a highly lucrative peace treaty with them, extracting massive gold and GPT tribute to fund our total war against Russia.
            - Public: -30
            - Private: -30
            - TargetID: 0
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #4:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: sZpnIkFpcW1b6oRP
          - toolName: set-flavors
          - input:
            - Rationale: We must urgently address our -1378 GPT deficit to prevent science penalties and unit disbandment. By maintaining Gold and Production at 100 but lowering Science to 60, we encourage our AI to run 'Infinite Project: Wealth' instead of 'Infinite Project: Research' in our highly productive cities, balancing the budget. We keep Offense at 100, Espionage at 100, and Nuke/UseNuke high to continue our aggressive stance against Russia and defend against their science lead, while using our massive air force (Air at 100) to dominate the skies.
            - Flavors:
              - Defense: 70
              - Naval: 100
              - Nuke: 95
              - GreatPeople: 30
              - NavalRecon: 100
              - Mobilization: 90
              - Air: 100
              - Offense: 100
              - Spaceship: 0
              - Happiness: 100
              - WaterConnection: 50
              - Antiair: 90
              - NavalGrowth: 50
              - Infrastructure: 100
